Fetches LaTeX source from arXiv (HTML or PDF as fallback, or a local file), writes a careful digest, then teaches in five modes (explain, explain+quiz, socratic, quiz, interrogate) at depth levels 1–10, logging progress so later sessions resume where they left off. Use when the user gives an arXiv id or URL, or says "explain this paper", "quiz me on", "socratic tutorial on", "I have questions about &lt;paper&gt;", "let's continue with &lt;paper&gt;", "teach me &lt;paper&gt;", or runs /paper-tutor. ([moritzhauschulz/paper-tutor](https://github.com/moritzhauschulz/paper-tutor/tree/HEAD/skills/paper-tutor))
- **paper-tutor**: Designed for researchers in **Applied AI, drug discovery, ML**, and any technical field requiring deep paper comprehension. ([shivashankarv/journal-club](https://github.com/shivashankarv/journal-club/tree/HEAD/skills/paper-tutor))
- **paper-tutor**: Guide interactive paper reading through short explanations, one question at a time, glossary cards, spaced review and a one-page note. Use when the user says walk me through this paper, quiz me one question at a time, don't summarize it, or 交互式精读、论文带读、带我读、一问一答、考我、别直接总结、我回 1/2/3 就行. Not for generating HTML/PDF; use paper-reader for that. ([Yuwangzhi/paper-reading-skills](https://github.com/Yuwangzhi/paper-reading-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/paper-tutor))
- **paper-tutor-report**: Runs an interactive paper walkthrough from the start - the agent teaches the paper stage by stage, answers the user's follow-up questions, keeps a distilled paper card on disk, then writes a focused standalone Chinese paper report. Use when the user hands over a paper (PDF dragged in as an attachment, local PDF path, arXiv link, or PDF URL) and wants it explained or read together, or mentions 带我读论文, 讲解这篇论文, 精读, 共读, 边读边问, 读完出报告, paper-tutor-report. For an already-finished chat transcript exported to markdown, use paper-chat-report instead. ([HardingPan/paper-tutor-report](https://github.com/HardingPan/paper-tutor-report/tree/HEAD/paper-tutor-report))
- **paper-tutorial-briefing**: Turn an AI paper into a rigorous but readable tutorial briefing. The goal is not to paraphrase the abstract; the goal is to help a researcher understand what the paper changes, what evidence supports it, and what should be tried locally next. ([StaryMoon/ai-researcher-skills](https://github.com/StaryMoon/ai-researcher-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/paper-tutorial-briefing))
- **paper-tweet-generator**: Generates a structured reading tweet from an academic paper (PDF, Word, or Text), highlighting specific product advantages. Use when the user wants to turn a document into a social media post or reading summary. ([aipoch/medical-research-skills](https://github.com/aipoch/medical-research-skills/tree/HEAD/scientific-skills/Other/paper-tweet-generator) · ★2.1k)
- **paper-twin-rewriting**: Deep template-guided academic rewriting for TeX manuscripts. Use when the user wants Codex to rewrite, not merely polish, one or more specified sections of a manuscript .tex file by imitating the writing paradigm, section organization, formula exposition, paragraph rhythm, and language distribution of one or more template papers supplied as .tex or Markdown. Trigger for requests such as paper twin rewriting, template-guided rewriting, imitate this paper's writing style, rewrite this TeX section using template papers, or deep academic style transfer for a manuscript section. Use when a video needs paper-unfold reveals in many variations (different ball shapes, accordion, folded note, scroll, sticky notes) or writing that unfolds with the sheet; for the most photo-real single reveal prefer the paper-unfold-frames skill.
